    Source: MOONData2627.MenuHelp (9,212 rows)
    69 distinct users | CompCodes: '2' (primary), 'NU' (second property)

    ┌──────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │ Column   │ Meaning                                                  │
    ├──────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │ Option   │ Menu/screen caption (matches window titles)              │
    │ OPT1     │ Module id (11=Finance, 16=Inventory, 17=POS, 19=GST)   │
    │ OPT2     │ Level-2 group id (0 = top-level item)                   │
    │ OPT3     │ Level-3 item id (0 = group header)                      │
    │ OPT4     │ Level-4 (rare)                                          │
    │ Flag     │ E=Executable, R=Report, N=Group/header, V=void/hidden   │
    └──────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Flag Semantics:
    ┌──────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  E   │ Executable screen — user can open and use this form         │
    │  R   │ Report — user can run this report                           │
    │  N   │ Group/header — container for submenu items (not clickable)  │
    │  V   │ Void/hidden — invisible to this user                        │
    └──────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Per-User Menu Trees:
    ┌────────────┬───────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────┐

================================================================================
    [SECTION 9] ERROR HANDLING PATTERNS  [VERIFIED-VB6]
================================================================================

    ┌──────────────────────────────┬───────┬─────────────────────────────────┐
    │ Pattern                      │ Count │ Impact                          │
    ├──────────────────────────────┼───────┼─────────────────────────────────┤
    │ On Error Resume Next         │  88   │ Errors silently swallowed;      │
    │                              │       │ app continues in bad state     │
    │ Missing RollbackTrans        │  15+  │ Partial posting possible;       │
    │                              │       │ data corruption risk            │
    │ MoveNext without EOF guard   │  30+  │ Runtime error 3021 (BOF/EOF)    │
    │ Division by zero             │  50+  │ Runtime error 11; form crash    │
    │ Kill without error handling  │   6   │ Runtime error 53 (File not found)│
    │ Hardcoded dates "01/01/99"   │   2   │ Edge case calculation errors    │
    │ Objects not set to Nothing   │ ~100  │ Memory leaks over time          │
    │                              │  vs   │                                 │
    │                              │ 1000s │                                 │
    └──────────────────────────────┴───────┴─────────────────────────────────┘

    Error Handling Flow (typical VB6 form):
    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  Private Sub Form_Load()                                            │
    │      On Error Resume Next          ← Swallows ALL errors silently    │
    │      ' ... load data ...                                           │
    │      ' No error checking                                            │
    │  End Sub                                                            │
    │                                                                     │
    │  Private Sub cmdSave_Click()                                        │
    │      On Error Resume Next          ← No rollback on failure          │
    │      ' ... save data ...                                            │
    │      ' No validation                                                │
    │  End Sub                                                            │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    OWASP Mapping:
    ├── A05:2021 — Security Misconfiguration (On Error Resume Next)
    ├── A08:2021 — Data Integrity Failures (missing transactions)
    └── A09:2021 — Logging Failures (silent error swallowing)

================================================================================
    [SECTION 10] TRANSACTION HANDLING  [VERIFIED-VB6]
================================================================================

    Transaction Pattern in VB6 (client-side ADODB):
    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  cn.BeginTrans                                                      │
    │  ' ... multiple INSERT/UPDATE statements ...                        │
    │  If success Then                                                   │
    │      cn.CommitTrans                                                 │
    │  Else                                                               │
    │      cn.RollbackTrans    ← MISSING in 15+ posting routines!         │
    │  End If                                                             │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Locations with Transaction Issues:
    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6241-6247: KOT posting (no rollback)              │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6449-6491: Bill posting (no rollback)             │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6499-6515: Settlement (no rollback)               │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6520-6579: Check-out posting (no rollback)        │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6581-6601: Payment (no rollback)                  │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6602-6627: Transfer (no rollback)                │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6628-6646: Adjustment (no rollback)              │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6647-6662: Refund (no rollback)                  │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6665-6682: Night Audit (no rollback)             │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6683-6688: NA posting (no rollback)               │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6697-6704: Revenue (no rollback)                  │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6711-6733: Room charge (no rollback)              │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6740-6764: POS sale (no rollback)                 │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6781-6798: Inventory (no rollback)                │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6804-6831: Purchase (no rollback)                 │
    │ EXTRAS.text Lines 6832-6856: Stock transfer (no rollback)           │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Risk: If any statement fails mid-transaction, partial data committed.
    Modernization: Server-side transactions with proper rollback handling.

================================================================================
    [SECTION 11] JOB SCHEDULER  [VERIFIED-SQL]
================================================================================

    Table: JobSchedule + JobScheduledDetail
    Polls: ~5,214 calls per session (MOST polled table in HMS)
    Frequency: Every ~1 second (background timer)

    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  Select JS.*,JD.Job_Id,JD.JobName                                   │
    │  From JobSchedule JS                                                │
    │  Inner Join JobScheduledDetail JD On JS.Schedule_Id=JD.Schedule_Id │
    │  Where JS.LogSite_Code='KK'                                         │
    │  And '28/Sep/2026' Between JS.ScheduleStartDate And JS.ScheduleEndDate│
    │  And JS.ScheduleEnabled=1                                            │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Job Types:
    ├── SMS sending (DBSendSMS outbox processing)
    ├── Night Audit auto-run
    ├── Report printing
    └── Data sync/backup

    Key Queries:
    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │ Poll: Select * from JOBScheduledDetail                              │
    │       Where JobEnabled=1 And JobScheduledOn Is Not Null             │
    │ Date: Select Convert(Varchar,GetDate(),120)                         │
    │ List: Select Schedule_Id,ScheduleName From JobSchedule              │
    │       Where ScheduleEnabled=1 And LOGSITE_CODE='KK'                 │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Modernization: Replace with proper job scheduler (Hangfire/Quartz.NET)

================================================================================
    [SECTION 12] SMS/MESSAGING FLOW  [VERIFIED-SQL]
================================================================================

    Table: DBSendSMS (18,810 rows)
    Poll: ~1,048 calls per session

    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

    │ TC-005 │ Posting      │ Charge then payment (Positive)               │
    │ TC-006 │ Posting      │ Duplicate-key regression (Bug regression)    │
    │ TC-007 │ Check-Out    │ Settlement & bill print (Positive)           │
    │ TC-008 │ Night Audit  │ Roll on clean data (Positive) — TEST DB ONLY │
    │ TC-009 │ Login        │ Inactive user (Negative)                     │
    │ TC-010 │ Login        │ Hardcoded backdoor (Security regression)     │
    │ TC-011 │ Reports      │ Report path drift (Negative)                 │
    │ TC-012 │ Permissions  │ AEDP enforcement                             │
    └────────┴──────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    Status: ALL NOT EXECUTED (need test DB copy of MOONData2627)
    Safety: No destructive tests against production data

    [SECTION 29] MODULE-WISE OPERATIONS (CRUD with SQL)

    Evidence: [VERIFIED-SQL] SQL_DEEP_MAPPING.md (17,525 statements) + module_manual.md

    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │ FRONT OFFICE OPERATIONS                                                     │
    ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Check-In (Walk-in)                                               │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   INSERT INTO GuestFolio (DocId, Vtype, VNo, Vprefix, Vdate, GuestProf,  │
    │ │     MFolioNo, BookingDocid, RoomNo, RoomCat, RoomType, RateCode,         │
    │ │     RoomRate, ChkInDate, ChkInTime, DepDate, DepTime, Adult, Children,   │
    │ │     PlanCode, PlanAmt, IncInRate, RRTaxInc, RRServiceChrg, U_Name,       │
    │ │     U_EntDt, U_AE, Site_Code, LogSite_Code)                              │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, @Vtype, @VNo, @Vprefix, @Vdate, @GuestProf, ...)       │
    │ │   INSERT INTO RoomOcc (DocId, SNo, FolioNo, RoomNo, RoomCat, RoomType,   │
    │ │     RateCode, RoomRate, ChkInDate, ChkInTime, DepDate, DepTime,         │
    │ │     Adult, Children, Type, U_Name, U_EntDt, U_AE, Site_Code, LogSite_Code)│
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, 1, @FolioNo, @RoomNo, @RoomCat, @RoomType, ...)        │
    │ ├── Validation: Guest exists? Room available? Rate valid?                  │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def check_in(doc_id: str, guest_prof: str, room_no: str,              │
    │ │               room_cat: str, room_type: str, rate_code: str,             │
    │ │               room_rate: float, adult: int, children: int,               │
    │ │               dep_date: datetime, user_name: str, site_code: str) -> dict │
    │ └── Tables: GuestFolio, RoomOcc, GuestProf, RoomMast, RoomCat             │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Check-Out                                                       │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   UPDATE RoomOcc SET Type='O', ChkOutDate=@date, ChkOutTime=@time,      │
    │ │     ChkoutUser=@user, UserchkoutDate=@date                               │
    │ │   WHERE DocId=@doc_id AND SNo=1 AND Type='I'                             │
    │ │   UPDATE RoomMast SET roomStat='D' WHERE Code=@room_no                   │
    │ ├── Validation: Folio exists? Already checked out?                         │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def check_out(doc_id: str, room_no: str, user_name: str,              │
    │ │               checkout_date: datetime, checkout_time: str) -> dict       │
    │ └── Tables: RoomOcc, RoomMast, GuestFolio                                  │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Post Charge                                                     │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   INSERT INTO PayCharge (DocId, SNo, FolioNo, FolioNoDocid, Bill_No,     │
    │ │     Vtype, VNo, Vprefix, Vdate, ContraDocId, PayCode, PayType,          │
    │ │     AmtDr, AmtCr, SettleDate, RestCode, SunCode, ROff, SunAppDate,      │
    │ │     RevCode, U_Name, U_EntDt, U_AE, Site_Code, LogSite_Code)            │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, @SNo, @FolioNo, @FolioNoDocid, @Bill_No, ...)         │
    │ ├── Validation: Folio exists? Amount > 0? PayCode valid?                  │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def post_charge(doc_id: str, folio_no: str, pay_code: str,            │
    │ │                  pay_type: str, amount: float, rev_code: str,           │
    │ │                  rest_code: str, user_name: str, site_code: str) -> dict │
    │ └── Tables: PayCharge, GuestFolio, RevMast                                 │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Settlement (Payment)                                            │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   INSERT INTO PayCharge (DocId, SNo, FolioNo, FolioNoDocid, Bill_No,     │
    │ │     Vtype, VNo, Vprefix, Vdate, ContraDocId, PayCode, PayType,          │
    │ │     AmtDr, AmtCr, SettleDate, RestCode, SunCode, ROff, SunAppDate,      │
    │ │     RevCode, U_Name, U_EntDt, U_AE, Site_Code, LogSite_Code)            │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, @SNo, @FolioNo, @FolioNoDocid, @Bill_No, ...)         │
    │ │   -- AmtCr = payment amount (Cr = money in)                              │
    │ ├── Validation: Folio exists? Amount <= outstanding?                      │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def settle_payment(doc_id: str, folio_no: str, pay_code: str,         │
    │ │                      pay_type: str, amount: float, user_name: str,      │
    │ │                      site_code: str) -> dict                             │
    │ └── Tables: PayCharge, GuestFolio                                          │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Room Change                                                      │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   UPDATE RoomOcc SET NewRoomNo=@new_room, Reason=@reason,                │
    │ │     ChngDate=@date                                                       │
    │ │   WHERE DocId=@doc_id AND SNo=1                                          │
    │ │   INSERT INTO RoomOcc (DocId, SNo, FolioNo, RoomNo, ...)                │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, 2, @FolioNo, @new_room, ...)                          │
    │ ├── Validation: New room available? Same category?                         │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def change_room(doc_id: str, new_room_no: str, reason: str,           │
    │ │                   user_name: str, site_code: str) -> dict                │
    │ └── Tables: RoomOcc, RoomMast                                              │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Get Room Status                                                  │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   SELECT roommast.*, RoomCat.Name AS RoomCategory, RoomCat.ShortName     │
    │ │   FROM roommast INNER JOIN RoomCat ON (roommast.Type = RoomCat.Type)    │
    │ │   AND (roommast.RoomCat = RoomCat.Code)                                  │
    │ │   WHERE (ROOMMAST.LOGSITE_CODE=@site)                                     │
    │ │   AND (((roommast.Type)='RO' AND RoomMast.InclCount='Y'))                │
    │ │   ORDER BY roommast.Code                                                 │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def get_room_status(site_code: str) -> List[dict]                       │
    │ └── Tables: RoomMast, RoomCat                                              │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Get Occupied Rooms                                               │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   SELECT RoomOcc.DocId as Code, RoomOcc.RoomNo, RoomOcc.FolioNo         │
    │ │   FROM RoomOcc WHERE (LOGSITE_CODE=@site OR LOGSITE_CODE='HO')          │
    │ │   AND roomocc.type not in ('C','O') AND CHKOUTDATE IS NULL               │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def get_occupied_rooms(site_code: str) -> List[dict]                      │
    │ └── Tables: RoomOcc                                                        │
    │                                                                             │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    │ │     LogSite_Code, U_Name, U_EntDT, U_AE)                                 │

    │ ├── Validation: Booking exists? Not already cancelled?                     │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │

    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Advance Deposit                                                  │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   INSERT INTO PayChargeH (DocId, Vtype, VNo, Vdate, ContraDocId,         │
    │ │     AmtCr, U_Name, U_EntDt, U_AE, Site_Code, LogSite_Code)              │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, 'AD', @VNo, @Vdate, @ContraDocId, @AmtCr, ...)         │
    │ ├── Validation: Booking exists? Amount > 0?                                │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def advance_deposit(doc_id: str, amount: float, user_name: str,       │
    │ │                       site_code: str) -> dict                            │
    │ └── Tables: PayChargeH, Booking                                            │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Get Booking Advance                                              │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   SELECT Sum(AmtCr-AmtDr) AS Advance FROM PayChargeH                     │
    │ │   WHERE VType In ('AD','AR') AND ContraDocId=@doc_id                     │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def get_booking_advance(doc_id: str) -> float                           │
    │ └── Tables: PayChargeH                                                     │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Room Availability Check                                          │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   SELECT Sum(NoofRooms) As tRoomBusy FROM ((ViewBooking as B             │
    │ │     Left Join GuestFolio GF on GF.BookingDocid=B.DociD                  │
    │ │     And GF.BookingSno=B.SNo)                                             │
    │ │     Left Join RoomOcc RO on RO.Docid=GF.DocId)                           │
    │ │   Where B.LOGSITE_CODE=@site AND B.RoomCat=@room_cat                     │
    │ │   And (B.RoomType='RO' or B.RoomType is Null)                            │
    │ │   And @date >= B.ArrDate And @date < B.DepDate                           │
    │ │   AND B.Cancel='N' And RO.ChkInDate Is Null                              │
    │ │                                                                          │
    │ │   SELECT Sum(NoRooms) As tNoRooms From RoomCat                           │
    │ │   Where Type='RO' And LOGSITE_CODE=@site AND InclCount='Y'              │
    │ │   And Code=@room_cat                                                     │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def check_room_availability(site_code: str, room_cat: str,            │
    │ │                              date: datetime) -> dict                      │
    │ └── Tables: ViewBooking, GuestFolio, RoomOcc, RoomCat                      │
    │                                                                             │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    │ │   INSERT INTO Ledger (DocId, V_Date, SubCode, ContraSub, AmtCr, AmtDr,   │
    │ │     V_Type, V_No, v_Prefix, V_SNo, Chq_No, Chq_Date, Clg_Date,          │
    │ │     Narration, Site_Code, LogSite_Code, U_Name, U_EntDt, U_AE)          │
    │ │   VALUES (@DocId, @V_Date, @SubCode, @ContraSub, @AmtCr, @AmtDr, ...)   │
    │ ├── Validation: Dr = Cr (double-entry)? Accounts exist?                    │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def create_voucher(doc_id: str, vtype: str, vno: int, vdate: datetime,│
    │ │                     narration: str, entries: List[dict],                │
    │ │                     user_name: str, site_code: str) -> dict              │
    │ └── Tables: LedgerM, Ledger, SubGroup                                      │
    │                                                                             │
    │ OPERATION: Bank Reconciliation                                              │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   UPDATE Ledger SET Clg_Date=@clearing_date, Chq_No=@chq_no,            │
    │ │     Chq_Date=@chq_date                                                   │
    │ │   WHERE DocId=@doc_id AND V_SNo=@sno                                     │
    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def reconcile_cheque(doc_id: str, sno: int, chq_no: str,              │
    │ │                        chq_date: datetime, clearing_date: datetime)      │

    │ OPERATION: Get Ledger Balance                                               │
    │ ├── SQL:                                                                    │
    │ │   SELECT SUBCODE, SUM(CURR_BAL) AS CURRBAL FROM SUBGROUPCURRBAL          │

    │ ├── Python:                                                                 │
    │ │   def get_ledger_balance() -> List[dict]                                 │

    │ │   WHERE JS.LogSite_Code=@site                                              │
    │ │   AND @date BETWEEN JS.ScheduleStartDate AND JS.ScheduleEndDate          │

    │                                                                             │

    │ ├── Single → float                                                          │
    │ ├── Double → float                                                          │

    │ ├── Boolean → bool                                                           │
    │ ├── Currency → decimal.Decimal                                             │
    │ ├── Variant → Any                                                           │
    │ └── Object → Any                                                            │
    │                                                                             │
    │ VB6 Error Handling → Python Error Handling                                  │
    │ ├── On Error Resume Next → try/except (with logging!)                       │
    │ ├── On Error GoTo → try/except/finally                                      │
    │ ├── Err.Number → exception.__class__.__name__                              │
    │ ├── Err.Description → str(exception)                                        │
    │ └── Err.Raise → raise CustomException                                       │
    │                                                                             │
    │ VB6 SQL Pattern → Python SQL Pattern                                       │
    │ ├── String concatenation → Parameterized queries (SQLAlchemy text())       │
    │ │   VB6: "SELECT * FROM X WHERE Code='" + txtCode + "'"                   │
    │ │   Python: session.execute(text("SELECT * FROM X WHERE Code=:code"),      │
    │ │                        {"code": txt_code})                               │
    │ ├── Recordset loop → SQLAlchemy query iteration                            │
    │ ├── MoveFirst → query.first()                                               │
    │ ├── MoveNext → next(query)                                                  │
    │ ├── EOF → StopIteration                                                     │
    │ └── RecordCount → query.count()                                             │
    │                                                                             │
    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    │ # models/base.py                                                            │
    │ from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, String, Integer, Float,       │
    │ from sqlalchemy import DateTime, SmallInteger, Text, Numeric, ForeignKey    │

    │ from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship                        │
    │                                                                             │
    │ Base = declarative_base()                                                   │
    │                                                                             │
    │ class AuditMixin:                                                           │
    │     U_Name = Column(String(10))                                            │
    │     U_EntDt = Column(DateTime)                                              │
    │     U_AE = Column(String(1))  # 'A'/'E'/'D'                                │
    │     Site_Code = Column(String(2))                                          │
    │     LogSite_Code = Column(String(2))                                       │
    │                                                                             │
    │ class GuestFolio(Base, AuditMixin):                                         │
    │     __tablename__ = 'GuestFolio'                                           │
    │     DocId = Column(String(21), primary_key=True)                            │
    │     Vtype = Column(String(5))                                              │

    │ class RoomOcc(Base, AuditMixin):                                            │
    │     __tablename__ = 'RoomOcc'                                              │

    │                                                                             │
    │ revision = '001'                                                            │
    │ down_revision = None                                                        │

    │     # Add Foreign Keys (NOCHECK - app behavior preserved)                   │

    │ ├── Compatibility level 100 preserved                                       │
    │ └── No schema changes — app behavior preserved                              │
    │                                                                             │
    │ Phase 2: FK Add-Back (Week 3-4)                                             │
    │ ├── Add Foreign Keys (NOCHECK) via Alembic migration 001                   │
    │ ├── Add performance indexes via Alembic migration 002                       │
    │ ├── Verify app behavior unchanged (dual-run)                                │
    │ └── Rollback plan: Alembic downgrade                                        │
    │                                                                             │
    │ Phase 3: Python ORM Layer (Week 5-8)                                        │
    │ ├── SQLAlchemy models for all 277 tables                                    │
    │ ├── Repository pattern for data access                                      │
    │ ├── Service layer for business logic                                        │
    │ └── Unit tests (pytest)                                                     │
    │                                                                             │
    │ Phase 4: API Layer (Week 9-12)                                              │
    │ ├── FastAPI endpoints for all modules                                       │

    │ ├── Authorization (AEDP rights → claims)                                    │
    │ └── Integration tests                                                        │
    │                                                                             │
    │ Phase 5: Frontend (Week 13-20)                                              │
    │ ├── React/Next.js for all forms                                            │
    │ ├── Dual-run with VB6 (side-by-side verification)                           │
    │ └── Cutover when 100% match achieved                                        │
    │                                                                             │
